twin re-measure (R-BA57)

Every (scenario, n) whose twin A/A (BlockedArray.twin vs BlockedArray) read other than TIE on its crossover phase after the K passes was measured once more as a whole group (every label, K passes); the re-measured cells replace the first ones and every verdict of this report reads them. The first measurement is kept in results.json remeasure[]. min ratio = min-of-K twin / BlockedArray (R-BA41). A missing twin cell is never re-measured. None: every twin read TIE (or was missing) after the K passes. REPORTED.
